1	# Wan2GP Fork Migration — Design Document
2	
3	## 1. Executive Summary
4	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a flat vendored snapshot with no `.git` directory, so it cannot be cleanly rebased onto `deepbeepmeep/Wan2GP` even though it is roughly 50 days and about 9 minor versions behind. The hottest drift zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where 22 files differ and the working diff is roughly 4303 lines.
5	
6	This migration plan is intentionally a planning artifact, not an implementation batch. It breaks the migration into 4 sprints of 2 weeks each so the team can inventory drift first, cut over to a maintainable fork-plus-patch model second, resync the risky `models/ltx2/` surface third, and finish with sustainability controls last.
7	
8	The non-negotiable constraint is that no functionality may be lost during any sprint. The plan therefore treats these as protected behaviors that must survive with explicit verification: IC-LoRA auto-injection for pose, depth, canny, and cameraman modes; the newly landed `ltx_anchor` kind; the existing `ltx_control` pixel cross-fade guide-video path; the `ltx_hybrid` route; `clear_conditioning` keyframe-token stripping before VAE decode; the existing 6-file travel test suite; and the import-path contract that supports both dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers and bare-module callers.
9	
10	## 2. Current-State Inventory
11	
12	### 2a. Vendored tree facts
13	`reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` is currently a plain copied tree rather than a repository checkout, so there is no `.git` metadata to rebase, cherry-pick, or diff-history against. The vendored changelog currently tops out at `WanGP v10.01` dated January 1, 2026, while upstream context for this plan is `v10.951` dated February 19, 2026.
14	
15	The hottest resync zone is `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/`, where the working comparison indicates 22 differing files and about 4303 lines of diff. That is large enough that the migration cannot safely be framed as a single blind replacement.
16	
17	The repo also has a non-trivial import and runtime surface already coupled to the current mount path. The approved background for this plan treats that as a 26+ call-site contract, which is why the migration must preserve both filesystem location and import semantics rather than just swapping in a dependency reference.
18	
19	### 2b. Genuinely missing upstream items
20	The current vendored tree is missing several upstream features that are desired outcomes of the migration rather than already-carried drift. Confirmed missing items include `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_pipelines/utils/res2s.py` and `Wan2GP/defaults/ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json`.
21	
22	Other upstream-side capabilities that this design document should plan to absorb during the migration include `_apply_gamma_to_media`, `LTX2_OUTPAINT_GAMMA`, `LTX2_DISABLE_STAGE2_WITH_CONTROL_VIDEO`, and the newer prompt-enhancer improvements. These belong in the migration inventory because they are feature gaps, not just cleanup opportunities.
23	
24	### 2c. Already-vendored drift-only items
25	`Wan2GP/shared/utils/self_refiner.py` is already vendored in the current tree. It should therefore be treated as a drift candidate that may need reconciliation with upstream head, not as a missing upstream backfill.
26	
27	That distinction matters for sprint design: Sprint 2 can absorb `self_refiner` drift as a low-risk resync task, while `res2s.py` and `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` remain true additions.
28	
29	### 2d. Patch seam and lifecycle
30	The repo already has an established patch seam for Wan2GP-specific runtime behavior. The module-load trigger is `source/__init__.py:6`, which ensures `source.models.wgp.wgp_patches` is importable during bootstrap.
31	
32	Actual patch application happens later in the runtime lifecycle at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`, where `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` is called after the Wan2GP module is loaded and before orchestration proceeds. The migration plan should preserve this existing seam instead of introducing a second patch package.
33	
34	### 2e. banodoco-carry items
35	Some behaviors are not generic upstream drift and must be treated as load-bearing banodoco carry items until they are either upstreamed or deliberately relocated. The clearest examples are `clear_conditioning` in `Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py:66-79`, the `_IC_LORA_BY_MODE` registry in `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py`, and the `ltx_anchor`-related orchestrator rewiring around `_build_segment_anchor_guidance_config`, PATH A skip behavior, and segment-local anchor remapping.
36	
37	These items are the reason the migration cannot be framed as “replace vendor tree with upstream head.” Each one needs an explicit carry-location decision later in the plan.
38	
39	### 2f. Pre-existing test coverage gaps
40	The current test suite does not yet pin the cameraman-specific override branch or the task-registry dedup branch. T1 confirmed that current collected cameraman coverage is limited to `test_parse_ltx_control_cameraman` and `test_needs_ic_lora[cameraman-True]`, and neither one reaches `get_ic_lora_entry()`.
41	
42	That leaves the cameraman override branch at `source/core/params/travel_guidance.py:310-313` and the dedup logic at `source/task_handlers/tasks/task_registry.py:964-968` unexercised by current tests. Sprint 3 therefore needs new first-class test deliverables rather than relying on `-k` selectors over the existing suite.
43	
44	### 2g. Stale-doc inventory
45	T1 identified existing documentation that still describes `Wan2GP/` as a flat vendored tree prior to Sprint 2. Confirmed narrow-sweep hits are `README.md:39`, `STRUCTURE.md:22`, and `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:202`.
46	
47	The broader sweep also found `STRUCTURE.md:70`, which still describes `Wan2GP/` as “Upstream video generation engine (vendored, do-not-edit-in-place).” Sprint 2 therefore needs a doc-sweep gate that covers both the narrow regex and the broader `Wan2GP` plus `vendor` pass, followed by manual reviewer confirmation because the regex is heuristic.
48	
49	### 2h. Invocation contract
50	The repo’s test and worker invocation contract is `uv run --python 3.10 pytest …`, consistent with the locked Python 3.10 environment in `uv.lock` and the worker run examples in `docs/KIJAI_SVI_IMPLEMENTATION.md:195`. This document should treat that as the canonical pytest invocation form for every later verification gate.
51	
52	Plain `pytest` is not sufficient in this repo because it can fail with `ModuleNotFoundError: source` when the expected environment contract is not active. The migration plan therefore needs to standardize on the `uv run --python 3.10` prefix anywhere it names pytest.
53	
54	### 2i. Path-contract verification technique
55	The path contract has two caller classes that must both survive the migration. First, `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` inserts the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root. Second, the more common dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers can work when either the repo root or `Wan2GP/` is on `sys.path`, so they are necessary but not sufficient proof that the mount contract remains correct.
56	
57	The stricter contract is driven by the bare-module callers in `vendor_imports.py:76-88`: `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`. Those class-b callers require the absolute `Wan2GP/` insertion specifically, which is why the Sprint 2 smoke must assert `os.path.abspath(str(get_wan2gp_path())) in [os.path.abspath(p) for p in sys.path]` with no repo-root fallback.
58	
59	Bare-import reachability should be verified only through filesystem proxies such as `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py')`, `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py')`, and `os.path.isfile('Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py')`. `find_spec` and real imports on `shared.utils.*` are intentionally avoided because `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py` eagerly imports SciPy-sensitive solver modules. Full end-to-end validation of the bare-module callers should then come from the existing test suite exercising `vendor_imports.py` through normal codepaths.
60	
61	## 3. Fork Architecture Decisions
62	The migration will use the hybrid architecture defined by SD-001: `banodoco/Wan2GP` becomes the long-running fork, and `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` becomes a git submodule pinned to that fork rather than a flat copied tree. That choice keeps the existing filesystem contract stable while making future rebases, drift review, and upstream comparison mechanically possible again.
63	
64	Patch ownership is split deliberately rather than informally. SD-002 defines the required triage buckets so every deviation is classified as accept-upstream, upstreamable, banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch, or cruft; SD-003 stages the resync by subsystem so low-risk `shared/` and `postprocessing/` work lands before the hot `models/ltx2/` zone; and SD-004 keeps LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion out of the migration path so the migration is not coupled to a defaults experiment.
65	
66	Feature exposure is also staged. SD-005 keeps `res_2s` and `self_refiner` behind off-by-default flags until the fork relationship is stable, and SD-006 keeps any post-gen detailer work outside this initiative so the migration remains focused on source-of-truth and drift control instead of product expansion.
67	
68	Sustainability is handled as part of the architecture, not as follow-up cleanup. SD-007 sets a monthly rebase cadence with event-triggered syncs on upstream minor-version bumps, while SD-008 adds scheduled drift detection in CI so the repository does not slide back into another opaque vendor snapshot. Together, SD-001 through SD-008 define a maintainable fork-plus-patch-layer model rather than a one-time resync.
69

206	
207	## 6. Risk Register
208	| Risk | Sprint | Prob | Impact | Mitigation |
209	| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
210	| Triage misclassifies a banodoco-specific hunk as safe upstream drift | S1 | M | H | Spot-check hot-zone rows and require a second-pass review on `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` before Sprint 2 starts |
211	| Silent bare-module caller break: `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` regresses to insert the repo root, so dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers still work while `vendor_imports.py:76-88` callers break | S2 | M | H | Strict path smoke asserts `get_wan2gp_path()` absolute path on `sys.path` with no repo-root fallback, plus filesystem assertions for `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_handler.py`, `Wan2GP/models/qwen/qwen_main.py`, and `Wan2GP/shared/utils/loras_mutipliers.py` |
212	| Submodule mount breaks `Wan2GP.*` imports or the `Wan2GP/` filesystem path contract | S2 | M | H | Enforce the SD-001 mount-path invariant and re-run the strict path-contract smoke after cut-over |
213	| `self_refiner` version bump silently changes LTX-2 behavior | S2 | M | M | Compare against the pre-sprint behavioral baseline using the planned LTX-2 smoke |
214	| Stale doc wording survives the cut-over and misleads later maintainers | S2 | L | M | Run both narrow and broad regex sweeps plus a reviewer manual pass, explicitly acknowledging the regex sweep is heuristic |
215	| Verification commands fail the repo environment contract and produce misleading results | Any | M | H | Require `uv run --python 3.10 pytest` for every named pytest invocation |
216	| SciPy trigger via `shared.utils.*` smoke: a verification command imports or `find_spec`s `shared.utils.*` and trips `Wan2GP/shared/utils/__init__.py:1-3` | S2 | L | M | Keep the path smoke limited to filesystem checks and `sys.path` assertions only; never use `import` or `find_spec` on `shared.utils.*` |
217	| `clear_conditioning` is silently reverted during the `models/ltx2/` resync | S3 | M | H | Preserve it explicitly as bucket `(c)` carry and gate Sprint 3 on a byte-compare latent fixture |
218	| IC-LoRA registry collides with upstream union-control preload behavior | S3 | H | M | Pin both branches with the new override and fallback tests before accepting the hot-zone resync |
219	| Cameraman override or dedup regression slips through because the current suite never exercised those branches | S3 | H | H | Write `tests/test_travel_guidance_ic_lora_override.py` and `tests/test_task_registry_ic_lora_dedup.py` as first-class Sprint 3 deliverables |
220	| Parallel patch package or split patch registry emerges during consolidation | S4 | M | M | Keep all overrides in `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` per SD-001 and assert a single registry in the patch-state smoke |
221	| Patch smoke is anchored at the wrong lifecycle point and reports false confidence | S4 | M | M | Drive bootstrap to `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266` before inspecting patch state |
222	| Monthly rebase cadence slips and drift starts accumulating again | Ongoing | M | M | Use SD-008 weekly drift CI to surface missed cadence before drift becomes opaque again |
223	
224	## 7. Success Criteria
225	### Migration complete
226	- The `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/` submodule pointer is at upstream HEAD or within the monthly SD-007 cadence window.
227	- The 6 named regression suites plus the new Sprint 3 IC-LoRA tests all pass under `uv run --python 3.10 pytest`.
228	- Drift CI is green.
229	- The post-bootstrap `get_wgp_patch_state()` smoke passes.
230	- The strict path-contract smoke passes, including the bare-import target filesystem assertions.
231	
232	### Migration in progress
233	- The submodule is mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`.
234	- A subset of subsystems has been synced, but each completed checkpoint still has green tests.
235	- The doc sweeps plus reviewer pass are green.
236	- The strict path smoke is green at the current checkpoint.
237	
238	### Regression observed
239	- Any row in Section 5 fails its named verification step.
240	- Any named regression suite fails.
241	- The path-contract smoke fails either because `get_wan2gp_path()` is not on `sys.path` exactly as required or because any bare-import target filesystem assertion fails.
242	- The doc sweep surfaces stale wording that is not explicitly approved for the current sprint.
243	
244	## 8. Non-Goals
245	- Flipping `ltx_anchor` to default
246	- Real-GPU empirical validation of the new defaults
247	- Upscaler-pass anchor re-injection
248	- Identity-drift fixes
249	
250	## 9. Settled Decisions
251	### Decision 1
252	- `id`: `SD-001`
253	- `load_bearing`: `true`
254	- `decision`: Use a hybrid fork shape: maintain `banodoco/Wan2GP` as the long-running fork and consume it in `reigh-worker` as a git submodule mounted at `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`.
255	  Path/import contract sub-clause:
256	  - The submodule mount path stays exactly `reigh-worker/Wan2GP/`; no relocation or rename.
257	  - `ensure_wan2gp_on_path()` must continue inserting the absolute `Wan2GP/` directory itself into `sys.path`, not the repo root or a parent/sibling path.
258	  - The bare-module callers in `source/runtime/wgp_ports/vendor_imports.py:76-88` must remain valid, explicitly including `models.qwen.qwen_handler`, `shared.utils.loras_mutipliers`, and `models.qwen.qwen_main`.
259	  - The dotted `Wan2GP.*` callers remain supported as the other caller class.
260	  - Deviations that do not belong in the fork stay in the existing `source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py` seam, which is module-loaded via `source/__init__.py:6` and applied by `apply_all_wgp_patches(wgp, self.wan_root)` at `source/models/wgp/orchestrator.py:265-266`.
261	  - No parallel runtime-side Wan2GP patch package is introduced.
262	- `rationale`: This preserves the current runtime contract while restoring maintainability. The stable mount path protects the import surface already used by both dotted `Wan2GP.*` imports and the bare-module import sites, and the existing patch seam prevents fork-only changes from being scattered into a second, conflicting patch registry.
263	
264	### Decision 2
265	- `id`: `SD-002`
266	- `load_bearing`: `true`
267	- `decision`: Every deviation between the current vendored tree and upstream is triaged into one of four buckets: `(a) accept-upstream`, `(b) upstreamable`, `(c) banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch`, `(d) cruft`. The canonical artifact is `docs/wan2gp-triage.csv` with columns `path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification`.
268	  Illustrative sample row:
269	
270	  ```csv
271	  path,hunk,bucket,carry_location,owner,verification
272	  Wan2GP/models/ltx2/ltx_core/tools.py,"clear_conditioning() strips appended keyframe tokens before VAE decode",banodoco-fork-or-banodoco-patch,banodoco/Wan2GP or source/models/wgp/wgp_patches.py,worker-runtime,byte-compare saved latent fixture
273	  ```
274	- `rationale`: The migration risk is concentrated in drift classification, not in git mechanics alone. A forced bucket per hunk makes carry decisions reviewable, gives Sprint 1 a concrete deliverable, and prevents load-bearing customizations like `clear_conditioning` from being lost inside an undifferentiated merge.
275	
276	### Decision 3
277	- `id`: `SD-003`
278	- `load_bearing`: `true`
279	- `decision`: Resync by subsystem, not all at once: `shared/` plus `postprocessing/` first, `models/ltx2/` second, and defaults/configs last.
280	- `rationale`: The repo already depends on fragile `models/ltx2/` behavior, including `ltx_anchor`, IC-LoRA handling, and `clear_conditioning`. Staging the migration lowers blast radius, lets the team validate path/import and doc updates before touching the 22-file hot zone, and keeps rollback boundaries understandable.
281	
282	### Decision 4
283	- `id`: `SD-004`
284	- `load_bearing`: `false`
285	- `decision`: Hold LTX 2.3 distilled 1.1 default promotion for a separate A/B sprint outside the migration, even if `ltx2_22B_distilled_1_1.json` is imported during the resync.
286	- `rationale`: Default promotion is a product-choice experiment, not a prerequisite for establishing the fork relationship. Separating it from the migration keeps “source sync” and “new default behavior” from failing together or obscuring regressions.
287	
288	### Decision 5
289	- `id`: `SD-005`
290	- `load_bearing`: `false`
291	- `decision`: Introduce `res_2s` and refreshed `self_refiner` support behind off-by-default flags during Sprints 2 and 3, with any default promotion deferred until upstream behavior and local verification converge.
292	- `rationale`: Both features are desirable, but neither is required to complete the vendor-to-fork transition safely. Flagging them keeps the migration forward-moving without forcing immediate behavior changes in the travel pipeline.
293	
294	### Decision 6
295	- `id`: `SD-006`
296	- `load_bearing`: `false`
297	- `decision`: Keep post-gen detailer work out of this migration plan and treat it as a separate initiative after the fork-plus-patch relationship is stable.
298	- `rationale`: The user preference already points toward a task-handler implementation rather than inline travel-pipeline detailer logic. Folding that feature into the migration would expand scope, confuse verification ownership, and make it harder to distinguish migration regressions from brand-new behavior.
299	
300	### Decision 7
301	- `id`: `SD-007`
302	- `load_bearing`: `true`
303	- `decision`: Rebase the fork monthly, with additional event-triggered syncs whenever upstream ships a new minor version that touches relevant subsystems.
304	- `rationale`: Weekly rebases would create churn without matching the repo’s current staffing and risk profile, while ad hoc syncs are what created the current opaque drift. Monthly cadence plus event triggers is frequent enough to keep diffs reviewable and infrequent enough to be realistic.
305	
306	### Decision 8
307	- `id`: `SD-008`
308	- `load_bearing`: `true`
309	- `decision`: Add `.github/workflows/wan2gp-drift.yml` as a weekly scheduled CI job that compares the pinned submodule pointer against upstream HEAD and reports drift for review.
310	- `rationale`: Once the vendor tree becomes a submodule, drift becomes measurable. A scheduled CI check converts that measurability into an operating habit, so the repo does not return to a silent `.git`-less copy that is only audited after major divergence has already accumulated.
311
```

1	"""
2	WGP monkeypatches for headless operation.
3	
4	These patches adapt WGP (Wan2GP) for headless/programmatic use without

16	from source.core.log import model_logger
17	from source.runtime.wgp_bridge import (

27	_DEFAULT_PATCH_CONTEXT_ID = "default"
28	_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, object]]]] = {}
29	_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS: dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, object]]] = {}
30	
31	
32	def _normalize_patch_context_id(context_id: str | None = None) -> str:
33	    normalized = context_id or _DEFAULT_PATCH_CONTEXT_ID
34	    return str(normalized)
35	
36	
37	def _context_patch_state(context_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, object]]]:
38	    return _PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.setdefault(_normalize_patch_context_id(context_id), {})
39	
40	
41	def _context_patch_rollbacks(context_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
42	    return _PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS.setdefault(_normalize_patch_context_id(context_id), {})
43	
44	
45	def _register_patch_application(
46	    patch_name: str,
47	    target_key: str,
48	    *,
49	    applied: bool,
50	    context_id: str | None = None,
51	    rollback=None,
52	) -> None:
53	    state_bucket = _context_patch_state(context_id).setdefault(patch_name, {})
54	    state_bucket[target_key] = {"applied": bool(applied)}
55	    if rollback is not None:
56	        rollback_bucket = _context_patch_rollbacks(context_id).setdefault(patch_name, {})
57	        rollback_bucket[target_key] = rollback
58	
59	
60	def get_wgp_patch_state(*, context_id: str | None = None) -> dict[str, dict[str, dict[str, object]]]:
61	    state = _PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.get(_normalize_patch_context_id(context_id), {})
62	    return {
63	        patch_name: {
64	            target_key: dict(metadata)
65	            for target_key, metadata in patch_entries.items()
66	        }
67	        for patch_name, patch_entries in state.items()
68	    }
69	
70	
71	def clear_wgp_patch_context(context_id: str | None = None) -> None:
72	    normalized = _normalize_patch_context_id(context_id)
73	    _PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.pop(normalized, None)
74	    _PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS.pop(normalized, None)
75	
76	
77	def rollback_wgp_patches(
78	    *,
79	    patch_name: str | None = None,
80	    target_key: str | None = None,
81	    context_id: str | None = None,
82	) -> int:
83	    if context_id is not None:
84	        contexts = [_normalize_patch_context_id(context_id)]
85	    else:
86	        contexts = sorted(set(_PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS.keys()) | set(_PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.keys()))
87	    restored = 0
88	
89	    for normalized_context in contexts:
90	        rollback_state = _PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS.get(normalized_context, {})
91	        state = _PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.get(normalized_context, {})
92	
93	        patch_names = [patch_name] if patch_name is not None else list(rollback_state.keys())
94	        for current_patch_name in patch_names:
95	            patch_rollbacks = rollback_state.get(current_patch_name, {})
96	            target_keys = [target_key] if target_key is not None else list(patch_rollbacks.keys())
97	            for current_target_key in target_keys:
98	                rollback = patch_rollbacks.pop(current_target_key, None)
99	                if callable(rollback):
100	                    restored += int(rollback())
101	                patch_state = state.get(current_patch_name, {})
102	                patch_state.pop(current_target_key, None)
103	                if not patch_state:
104	                    state.pop(current_patch_name, None)
105	            if not patch_rollbacks:
106	                rollback_state.pop(current_patch_name, None)
107	
108	        if not rollback_state:
109	            _PATCH_CONTEXT_ROLLBACKS.pop(normalized_context, None)
110	        if not state:
111	            _PATCH_CONTEXT_STATE.pop(normalized_context, None)
112	
113	    return restored

116	def apply_qwen_model_routing_patch(wgp_module: "types.ModuleType", wan_root: str) -> bool:
117	    """
118	    Patch WGP to route Qwen-family models to their dedicated handler.
119	
120	    Why: WGP's load_wan_model doesn't natively handle Qwen models.
121	    This routes Qwen-family models (qwen_image_edit, etc.) to the
122	    dedicated Qwen handler in models/qwen/qwen_handler.py.
123	
124	    Args:
125	        wgp_module: The imported wgp module
126	        wan_root: Path to Wan2GP root directory
127	
128	    Returns:
129	        True if patch was applied successfully, False otherwise
130	    """
131	    try:
132	        _orig_load_wan_model = wgp_module.load_wan_model
133	
134	        def _patched_load_wan_model(
135	            model_filename,
136	            model_type,
137	            base_model_type,
138	            model_def,
139	            quantizeTransformer=False,
140	            dtype=None,
141	            VAE_dtype=None,
142	            mixed_precision_transformer=False,
143	            save_quantized=False
144	        ):
145	            # Check if this is a Qwen-family model
146	            try:
147	                base = wgp_module.get_base_model_type(base_model_type)
148	            except (AttributeError, ValueError, TypeError):
149	                base = base_model_type
150	
151	            if isinstance(base, str) and "qwen" in base.lower():
152	                model_logger.debug_anomaly("QWEN_LOAD", "Routing to Qwen family loader via monkeypatch")
153	                _qwen_handler = get_qwen_family_handler()
154	                pipe_processor, pipe = _qwen_handler.load_model(
155	                    model_filename=model_filename,
156	                    model_type=model_type,
157	                    base_model_type=base_model_type,
158	                    model_def=model_def,
159	                    quantizeTransformer=quantizeTransformer,
160	                    text_encoder_quantization=wgp_module.text_encoder_quantization,
161	                    dtype=dtype,
162	                    VAE_dtype=VAE_dtype,
163	                    mixed_precision_transformer=mixed_precision_transformer,
164	                    save_quantized=save_quantized,
165	                )
166	                return pipe_processor, pipe
167	
168	            # Fallback to original WAN loader
169	            return _orig_load_wan_model(
170	                model_filename, model_type, base_model_type, model_def,
171	                quantizeTransformer=quantizeTransformer, dtype=dtype, VAE_dtype=VAE_dtype,
172	                mixed_precision_transformer=mixed_precision_transformer, save_quantized=save_quantized
173	            )
174	
175	        wgp_module.load_wan_model = _patched_load_wan_model
176	        return True
177	
178	    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as e:
179	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("QWEN_LOAD", f"Failed to apply load_wan_model patch: {e}")
180	        return False
181

183	def apply_qwen_lora_directory_patch(wgp_module: "types.ModuleType", wan_root: str) -> bool:
184	    """
185	    Patch get_lora_dir to redirect Qwen models to loras_qwen/ directory.
186	
187	    Why: Qwen models need their own LoRA directory (loras_qwen/) since they
188	    use a different LoRA format than WAN models.
189	
190	    Args:
191	        wgp_module: The imported wgp module
192	        wan_root: Path to Wan2GP root directory
193	
194	    Returns:
195	        True if patch was applied successfully, False otherwise
196	    """
197	    try:
198	        target_key = f"wgp_module:{id(wgp_module)}:get_lora_dir"
199	        existing = get_wgp_patch_state().get("qwen_lora_directory", {})
200	        if target_key in existing and getattr(wgp_module.get_lora_dir, "_headless_patch_target_key", None) == target_key:
201	            return True
202	
203	        _orig_get_lora_dir = wgp_module.get_lora_dir
204	
205	        def _patched_get_lora_dir(model_type: str):
206	            try:
207	                mt = (model_type or "").lower()
208	                if "qwen" in mt:
209	                    qwen_dir = os.path.join(wan_root, "loras_qwen")
210	                    if os.path.isdir(qwen_dir):
211	                        return qwen_dir
212	            except (ValueError, TypeError, OSError) as e:
213	                model_logger.debug_anomaly("QWEN_LOAD", f"Error checking Qwen LoRA directory for model_type={model_type}: {e}")
214	            return _orig_get_lora_dir(model_type)
215	
216	        _patched_get_lora_dir._headless_patch_name = "qwen_lora_directory"
217	        _patched_get_lora_dir._headless_patch_target_key = target_key
218	        wgp_module.get_lora_dir = _patched_get_lora_dir
219	        _register_patch_application(
220	            "qwen_lora_directory",
221	            target_key,
222	            applied=True,
223	            rollback=lambda: _rollback_qwen_lora_directory_patch(wgp_module, target_key, _orig_get_lora_dir),
224	        )
225	        return True
226	
227	    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as e:
228	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("QWEN_LOAD", f"Failed to apply get_lora_dir patch: {e}")
229	        return False

259	    Disable Qwen's built-in inpainting LoRA (preload_URLs) in headless mode.
260	
261	    Why: Qwen models have a built-in inpainting LoRA that gets auto-loaded.
262	    In headless mode, this can cause issues. Disabled by default unless
263	    HEADLESS_WAN2GP_ENABLE_QWEN_INPAINTING_LORA=1 is set.
264	
265	    Returns:
266	        True if patch was applied successfully, False otherwise
267	    """
268	    try:
269	        _qwen_main = get_qwen_main_module()
270	        _orig_qwen_get_loras_transformer = _qwen_main.model_factory.get_loras_transformer
271	
272	        def _patched_qwen_get_loras_transformer(self, get_model_recursive_prop, model_type, model_mode, **kwargs):
273	            try:
274	                if os.environ.get("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_ENABLE_QWEN_INPAINTING_LORA", "0") != "1":
275	                    return [], []
276	            except (ValueError, TypeError, OSError):
277	                # If env check fails, fall back to disabled behavior
278	                return [], []
279	            return _orig_qwen_get_loras_transformer(self, get_model_recursive_prop, model_type, model_mode, **kwargs)
280	
281	        _qwen_main.model_factory.get_loras_transformer = _patched_qwen_get_loras_transformer
282	        return True
283	
284	    except (ImportError, AttributeError, AssertionError, RuntimeError) as e:
285	        # AssertionError/RuntimeError: CUDA init fails on non-GPU machines during import
286	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("QWEN_LOAD", f"Failed to apply Qwen inpainting LoRA patch: {e}")
287	        return False
288	
289	
290	def apply_lora_key_tolerance_patch(wgp_module: "types.ModuleType") -> bool:
291	    """
292	    Patch LoRA loading to tolerate non-standard keys (e.g., lightx2v format).
293	
294	    Why: Some LoRA files (like lightx2v/Wan2.2-Distill-Loras) contain keys in
295	    non-standard formats (diff_m, norm_k_img) that mmgp rejects as hard errors.
296	    ComfyUI's loader silently skips these unrecognized keys. This patch wraps
297	    the LoRA preprocessor to strip unrecognized keys before mmgp's validator
298	    sees them, matching ComfyUI's behavior.
299	
300	    How: Monkeypatches wgp.get_loras_preprocessor to wrap the returned
301	    preprocessor with a key-stripping step that:
302	    1. Removes keys with no recognized LoRA suffix (handles diff_m, etc.)
303	    2. Removes keys whose module path doesn't exist in the model (handles
304	       norm_k_img and similar non-standard module references)
305	
306	    Args:
307	        wgp_module: The imported wgp module
308	
309	    Returns:
310	        True if patch was applied successfully, False otherwise
311	    """
312	    try:
313	        _orig_get_loras_preprocessor = wgp_module.get_loras_preprocessor
314	
315	        # Valid LoRA key suffixes that mmgp's validator recognizes
316	        # Order: longest first to avoid partial matches
317	        VALID_LORA_SUFFIXES = [
318	            '.lora_down.weight',
319	            '.lora_up.weight',
320	            '.lora_A.weight',
321	            '.lora_B.weight',
322	            '.dora_scale',
323	            '.diff_b',
324	            '.diff',
325	        ]
326	        KNOWN_PREFIXES = ("diffusion_model.", "transformer.")
327	
328	        def _patched_get_loras_preprocessor(transformer, model_type):
329	            orig_preproc = _orig_get_loras_preprocessor(transformer, model_type)
330	
331	            # Build set of known module names for unexpected-key detection
332	            modules_set = set(name for name, _ in transformer.named_modules())
333	
334	            def _tolerant_preprocessor(sd):
335	                # Run original preprocessor first (if any)
336	                if orig_preproc is not None:
337	                    sd = orig_preproc(sd)
338	
339	                # Detect prefix from first key (same logic as mmgp)
340	                first_key = next(iter(sd), None)
341	                if first_key is None:
342	                    return sd
343	
344	                pos = first_key.find(".")
345	                prefix = first_key[:pos + 1] if pos > 0 else ""
346	                if prefix not in KNOWN_PREFIXES:
347	                    prefix = ""
348	
349	                keys_to_remove = []
350	                for k in sd:
351	                    # Skip alpha keys (handled separately by mmgp before validation)
352	                    if k.endswith('.alpha'):
353	                        continue
354	
355	                    # Find matching suffix and extract module name
356	                    module_name = None
357	                    for suffix in VALID_LORA_SUFFIXES:
358	                        if k.endswith(suffix):
359	                            raw_module = k[:-len(suffix)]
360	                            if prefix and raw_module.startswith(prefix):
361	                                module_name = raw_module[len(prefix):]
362	                            else:
363	                                module_name = raw_module
364	                            break
365	
366	                    if module_name is None:
367	                        # No valid LoRA suffix found - would be invalid_keys in mmgp
368	                        keys_to_remove.append(k)
369	                        continue
370	
371	                    # Check if module exists in model - would be unexpected_keys in mmgp
372	                    if module_name not in modules_set:
373	                        keys_to_remove.append(k)
374	                        continue
375	
376	                if keys_to_remove:
377	                    model_logger.debug_anomaly(
378	                        "LORA_TOLERANCE",
379	                        f"Stripping {len(keys_to_remove)} non-standard/incompatible keys from LoRA: "
380	                        f"{keys_to_remove[:5]}{'...' if len(keys_to_remove) > 5 else ''}"
381	                    )
382	                    for k in keys_to_remove:
383	                        del sd[k]
384	
385	                return sd
386	
387	            return _tolerant_preprocessor
388	
389	        wgp_module.get_loras_preprocessor = _patched_get_loras_preprocessor
390	        return True
391	
392	    except (RuntimeError, AttributeError, ImportError) as e:
393	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("LORA_TOLERANCE", f"Failed to apply LoRA key tolerance patch: {e}")
394	        return False
395	
396	
397	def apply_lora_caching_patch() -> bool:
398	    """
399	    Cache LoRA loading between generations to avoid redundant reloads.
400	
401	    Why: WGP unloads LoRAs after every generation (wgp.py:6439) and mmgp's
402	    load_loras_into_model always unloads before loading. For a 20B Qwen model
403	    with CPU offloading, LoRA loading takes ~7 minutes per generation. When
404	    running the same LoRAs repeatedly (batch qwen_image tasks, travel segments),
405	    this is pure waste — 21 min of LoRA loading for 2.5 min of actual generation.
406	
407	    How:
408	    - Patches offload.unload_loras_from_model to be a no-op (keeps LoRAs loaded)
409	    - Patches offload.load_loras_into_model to skip if same LoRA files are
410	      already loaded on the model, otherwise does a real unload + load cycle
411	    - Cache is keyed on (model object identity, LoRA file paths)
412	    - Safety: checks model._loras_adapters to verify LoRAs are actually loaded
413	      (catches cases where error handlers cleared state)
414	
415	    Returns:
416	        True if patch was applied successfully, False otherwise
417	    """
418	    try:
419	        from mmgp import offload
420	
421	        target_key = f"mmgp.offload:{id(offload)}"
422	        existing = get_wgp_patch_state().get("lora_caching", {})
423	        if target_key in existing and getattr(offload.load_loras_into_model, "_headless_patch_target_key", None) == target_key:
424	            return True
425	
426	        _original_load = offload.load_loras_into_model
427	        _original_unload = offload.unload_loras_from_model
428	
429	        def _cached_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi=None, **kwargs):
430	            # check_only mode (used by LoRA validation) — pass through
431	            if kwargs.get("check_only", False):
432	                return _original_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi, **kwargs)
433	
434	            # Normalize to tuple for comparison
435	            paths = lora_path if isinstance(lora_path, list) else [lora_path]
436	            requested = tuple(paths)
437	
438	            # Check cache: same model object + same LoRA files + LoRAs actually loaded
439	            cached = getattr(model, "_headless_cached_lora_paths", None)
440	            if (
441	                cached is not None
442	                and cached == requested
443	                and getattr(model, "_loras_adapters", None) is not None
444	            ):
445	                model_logger.debug(
446	                    f"[LORA_CACHE] Skipping LoRA reload — same {len(requested)} LoRAs already loaded"
447	                )
448	                return
449	
450	            # Different LoRAs or first load — do full cycle
451	            if cached is not None:
452	                model_logger.debug(
453	                    f"[LORA_CACHE] LoRAs changed ({len(cached)} -> {len(requested)}), reloading"
454	                )
455	            else:
456	                model_logger.debug_anomaly("LORA_CACHE", f"First LoRA load: {len(requested)} LoRAs")
457	
458	            # Explicitly unload via original (our global patch is a no-op, and
459	            # the internal unload inside _original_load also hits our no-op,
460	            # so we must call the real one here)
461	            _original_unload(model)
462	
463	            result = _original_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi, **kwargs)
464	
465	            # Tag model with what we loaded
466	            model._headless_cached_lora_paths = requested
467	            return result
468	
469	        def _deferred_unload(model):
470	            """No-op — keeps LoRAs loaded for reuse by next generation."""
471	            if model is None:
472	                return
473	            model_logger.debug_anomaly("LORA_CACHE", "Deferring LoRA unload (cached for reuse)")
474	
475	        _cached_load._headless_patch_name = "lora_caching"
476	        _cached_load._headless_patch_target_key = target_key
477	        _deferred_unload._headless_patch_name = "lora_caching"
478	        _deferred_unload._headless_patch_target_key = target_key
479	        offload.load_loras_into_model = _cached_load
480	        offload.unload_loras_from_model = _deferred_unload
481	        _register_patch_application(
482	            "lora_caching",
483	            target_key,
484	            applied=True,
485	            rollback=lambda: _rollback_lora_caching_patch(offload, target_key, _original_load, _original_unload),
486	        )
487	        return True
488	
489	    except (ImportError, AttributeError, RuntimeError) as e:
490	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("LORA_CACHE", f"Failed to apply LoRA caching patch: {e}")
491	        return False
492	
493	
494	def apply_headless_app_stub(wgp_module: "types.ModuleType") -> bool:
495	    """
496	    Stub the 'app' global in wgp so plugin_manager checks are skipped.
497	
498	    Why: Upstream wgp.py references a global 'app' (WAN2GPApplication instance)
499	    in prepare_inputs_dict() for plugin hooks. 'app' is only created when wgp.py
500	    runs as __main__ (Gradio UI), not when imported. Without this stub,
501	    headless generation crashes with 'NameError: name app is not defined'.
502	
503	    The stub is a SimpleNamespace with no plugin_manager attribute, so
504	    hasattr(app, 'plugin_manager') returns False and the plugin path is skipped.
505	    """
506	    try:
507	        from types import SimpleNamespace
508	
509	        if not hasattr(wgp_module, "app"):
510	            wgp_module.app = SimpleNamespace()
511	            model_logger.debug_anomaly("WGP_PATCHES", "Stubbed wgp.app for headless mode (no plugin_manager)")
512	        return True
513	    except Exception as e:
514	        model_logger.warning(f"[WGP_PATCHES] Failed to stub wgp.app: {e}")
515	        return False
516	
517	
518	def _rollback_qwen_lora_directory_patch(wgp_module, target_key: str, original_get_lora_dir) -> int:
519	    if getattr(wgp_module.get_lora_dir, "_headless_patch_target_key", None) != target_key:
520	        return 0
521	    wgp_module.get_lora_dir = original_get_lora_dir
522	    return 1
523	
524	
525	def _rollback_lora_caching_patch(offload, target_key: str, original_load, original_unload) -> int:
526	    restored = 0
527	    if getattr(offload.load_loras_into_model, "_headless_patch_target_key", None) == target_key:
528	        offload.load_loras_into_model = original_load
529	        restored += 1
530	    if getattr(offload.unload_loras_from_model, "_headless_patch_target_key", None) == target_key:
531	        offload.unload_loras_from_model = original_unload
532	        restored += 1
533	    return restored
534	
535	
536	def apply_all_wgp_patches(
537	    wgp_module: "types.ModuleType",
538	    wan_root: str,
539	    *,
540	    context_id: str | None = None,
541	) -> dict:
542	    """
543	    Apply all WGP patches for headless operation.
544	
545	    Args:
546	        wgp_module: The imported wgp module
547	        wan_root: Path to Wan2GP root directory
548	
549	    Returns:
550	        Dict with patch names as keys and success status as values
551	    """
552	    results = {}
553	
554	    results["qwen_model_routing"] = apply_qwen_model_routing_patch(wgp_module, wan_root)
555	    results["qwen_lora_directory"] = apply_qwen_lora_directory_patch(wgp_module, wan_root)
556	    results["lora_multiplier_parser"] = apply_lora_multiplier_parser_patch(wgp_module)
557	    results["qwen_inpainting_lora"] = apply_qwen_inpainting_lora_patch()
558	    results["lora_key_tolerance"] = apply_lora_key_tolerance_patch(wgp_module)
559	    results["lora_caching"] = apply_lora_caching_patch()
560	    results["headless_app_stub"] = apply_headless_app_stub(wgp_module)
561	
562	    # Log summary
563	    successful = [k for k, v in results.items() if v]
564	    failed = [k for k, v in results.items() if not v]
565	
566	    if successful:
567	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("WGP_PATCHES", f"Applied: {', '.join(successful)}")
568	    if failed:
569	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("WGP_PATCHES", f"Failed (non-fatal): {', '.join(failed)}")
570	
571	    context_target_key = f"wgp_module:{id(wgp_module)}"
572	    for patch_name, applied in results.items():
573	        _register_patch_application(
574	            patch_name,
575	            context_target_key,
576	            applied=applied,
577	            context_id=context_id,
578	        )
579	
580	    return results
581

164	def upsert_wgp_model_definition(
165	    model_name: str,
166	    model_def: dict[str, Any],
167	    *,
168	    initialize: bool = False,
169	) -> dict[str, Any]:

177	    return final_model_def

184	class _RuntimePatchTransaction:
185	    def __init__(self, runtime, model_name: str, keys: tuple[str, ...]):
186	        self.runtime = runtime
187	        self.model_name = model_name
188	        self.keys = tuple(keys)
189	        self.models_def_target = runtime.models_def.setdefault(model_name, {})
190	        self.loaded_target = None
191	        if getattr(runtime, "transformer_type", None) == model_name and getattr(runtime, "wan_model", None) is not None:
192	            self.loaded_target = runtime.wan_model.model_def
193	        self.snapshot = {}
194	        for target_name, target in (("models_def", self.models_def_target), ("loaded_model", self.loaded_target)):
195	            if target is None:
196	                continue
197	            for key in self.keys:
198	                self.snapshot[(target_name, key)] = (key in target, target.get(key))
199	
200	
201	def begin_runtime_model_patch(model_name: str, *, keys: tuple[str, ...]):
202	    runtime = _get_runtime_for_mutation()
203	    return _RuntimePatchTransaction(runtime, model_name, keys)
204	
205	
206	def apply_runtime_model_patch(transaction: _RuntimePatchTransaction, patch_values: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
207	    for target in (transaction.models_def_target, transaction.loaded_target):
208	        if target is None:
209	            continue
210	        target.update(patch_values)
211	
212	
213	def rollback_runtime_model_patch(transaction: _RuntimePatchTransaction) -> None:
214	    for target_name, target in (("models_def", transaction.models_def_target), ("loaded_model", transaction.loaded_target)):
215	        if target is None:
216	            continue
217	        for key in transaction.keys:
218	            existed, previous = transaction.snapshot.get((target_name, key), (False, None))
219	            if existed:
220	                target[key] = previous
221	            else:
222	                target.pop(key, None)
223
```

3	    def clear_conditioning(self, latent_state: LatentState) -> LatentState:
4	        """
5	        Clear the conditioning from the latent state. This method removes extra tokens from the end of the latent.
6	        Therefore, conditioning items should add extra tokens ONLY to the end of the latent.
7	        """
8	        latent_state = latent_state.clone()
9	
10	        num_tokens = self.patchifier.get_token_count(self.target_shape)
11	        latent = latent_state.latent[:, :num_tokens]
12	        clean_latent = latent_state.clean_latent[:, :num_tokens]
13	        denoise_mask = torch.ones_like(latent_state.denoise_mask)[:, :num_tokens]
14	        positions = latent_state.positions[:, :, :num_tokens]
15	
16	        return LatentState(latent=latent, denoise_mask=denoise_mask, positions=positions, clean_latent=clean_latent)
17

1	"""Contracts for WGP patch idempotence metadata and rollback behavior."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	import sys
6	import types
7	
8	import source.models.wgp.wgp_patches as wgp_patches
9	
10	
11	def test_qwen_lora_directory_patch_is_idempotent_and_rollbackable(tmp_path):
12	    wgp_patches.rollback_wgp_patches()
13	
14	    qwen_dir = tmp_path / "loras_qwen"
15	    qwen_dir.mkdir()
16	    original_get_lora_dir = lambda _model_type: "default_lora_dir"
17	    fake_wgp_module = types.SimpleNamespace(get_lora_dir=original_get_lora_dir)
18	
19	    assert wgp_patches.apply_qwen_lora_directory_patch(fake_wgp_module, str(tmp_path)) is True
20	    first_wrapper = fake_wgp_module.get_lora_dir
21	    assert fake_wgp_module.get_lora_dir("qwen_image_edit") == str(qwen_dir)
22	
23	    assert wgp_patches.apply_qwen_lora_directory_patch(fake_wgp_module, str(tmp_path)) is True
24	    assert fake_wgp_module.get_lora_dir is first_wrapper
25	
26	    patch_state = wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state()["qwen_lora_directory"]
27	    target_key = next(iter(patch_state.keys()))
28	    assert patch_state[target_key]["applied"] is True
29	
30	    restored = wgp_patches.rollback_wgp_patches(
31	        patch_name="qwen_lora_directory",
32	        target_key=target_key,
33	    )
34	    assert restored == 1
35	    assert fake_wgp_module.get_lora_dir is original_get_lora_dir
36	
37	
38	def test_lora_caching_patch_is_idempotent_and_restorable(monkeypatch):
39	    wgp_patches.rollback_wgp_patches()
40	
41	    load_calls: list[tuple[object, tuple[str, ...]]] = []
42	    unload_calls: list[object] = []
43	
44	    def _original_load(model, lora_path, lora_multi=None, **kwargs):
45	        _ = (lora_multi, kwargs)
46	        paths = tuple(lora_path if isinstance(lora_path, list) else [lora_path])
47	        load_calls.append((model, paths))
48	        return "loaded"
49	
50	    def _original_unload(model):
51	        unload_calls.append(model)
52	        return None
53	
54	    fake_offload = types.SimpleNamespace(
55	        load_loras_into_model=_original_load,
56	        unload_loras_from_model=_original_unload,
57	    )
58	    fake_mmgp = types.ModuleType("mmgp")
59	    fake_mmgp.offload = fake_offload
60	    monkeypatch.setitem(sys.modules, "mmgp", fake_mmgp)
61	
62	    assert wgp_patches.apply_lora_caching_patch() is True
63	    patched_load = fake_offload.load_loras_into_model
64	    assert patched_load is not _original_load
65	
66	    model = types.SimpleNamespace(_loras_adapters=object())
67	    fake_offload.load_loras_into_model(model, ["a.safetensors"])
68	    fake_offload.load_loras_into_model(model, ["a.safetensors"])
69	
70	    assert len(load_calls) == 1
71	    assert len(unload_calls) == 1
72	
73	    assert wgp_patches.apply_lora_caching_patch() is True
74	    assert fake_offload.load_loras_into_model is patched_load
75	
76	    patch_state = wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state()["lora_caching"]
77	    target_key = next(iter(patch_state.keys()))
78	    restored = wgp_patches.rollback_wgp_patches(
79	        patch_name="lora_caching",
80	        target_key=target_key,
81	    )
82	    assert restored == 2
83	    assert fake_offload.load_loras_into_model is _original_load
84	    assert fake_offload.unload_loras_from_model is _original_unload
85

1	"""Contracts for runtime_registry patch transactions."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from types import SimpleNamespace
6	
7	from source.runtime.wgp_ports import runtime_registry
8	
9	
10	class _FakeRuntime:
11	    def __init__(self) -> None:
12	        self.models_def = {"model-a": {"existing": 1}}
13	        self.transformer_type = "model-a"
14	        self.wan_model = SimpleNamespace(model_def={"existing": 1})
15	        self.offloadobj = None
16	        self.reload_needed = False
17	
18	    def init_model_def(self, _model_name: str, model_def: dict[str, object]) -> dict[str, object]:
19	        initialized = dict(model_def)
20	        initialized["initialized"] = True
21	        return initialized
22	
23	
24	def test_runtime_model_patch_transaction_apply_and_rollback(monkeypatch) -> None:
25	    runtime = _FakeRuntime()
26	    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_registry, "get_wgp_runtime_module", lambda **_kwargs: runtime)
27	
28	    transaction = runtime_registry.begin_runtime_model_patch(
29	        "model-a",
30	        keys=("svi2pro", "sliding_window"),
31	    )
32	
33	    runtime_registry.apply_runtime_model_patch(
34	        transaction,
35	        {
36	            "svi2pro": True,
37	            "sliding_window": True,
38	        },
39	    )
40	
41	    assert runtime.models_def["model-a"]["svi2pro"] is True
42	    assert runtime.wan_model.model_def["sliding_window"] is True
43	
44	    runtime_registry.rollback_runtime_model_patch(transaction)
45	
46	    assert "svi2pro" not in runtime.models_def["model-a"]
47	    assert "sliding_window" not in runtime.wan_model.model_def
48	
49	
50	def test_upsert_model_definition_updates_loaded_model_for_active_transformer(monkeypatch) -> None:
51	    runtime = _FakeRuntime()
52	    monkeypatch.setattr(runtime_registry, "get_wgp_runtime_module", lambda **_kwargs: runtime)
53	
54	    result = runtime_registry.upsert_wgp_model_definition(
55	        "model-a",
56	        {"foo": "bar"},
57	        initialize=True,
58	    )
59	
60	    assert result["foo"] == "bar"
61	    assert result["initialized"] is True
62	    assert runtime.models_def["model-a"]["foo"] == "bar"
63	    assert runtime.wan_model.model_def["foo"] == "bar"
64

1	"""Contracts for context-scoped WGP patch registries."""
2	
3	from __future__ import annotations
4	
5	from types import SimpleNamespace
6	
7	from source.models.wgp import wgp_patches
8	
9	
10	def _make_dummy_wgp_module() -> SimpleNamespace:
11	    return SimpleNamespace(
12	        load_wan_model=lambda *args, **kwargs: (None, None),
13	        get_base_model_type=lambda model_type: model_type,
14	    )
15	
16	
17	def test_patch_state_is_context_scoped(monkeypatch) -> None:
18	    monkeypatch.setattr(wgp_patches, "apply_qwen_lora_directory_patch", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
19	    monkeypatch.setattr(wgp_patches, "apply_lora_multiplier_parser_patch", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
20	    monkeypatch.setattr(wgp_patches, "apply_qwen_inpainting_lora_patch", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
21	    monkeypatch.setattr(wgp_patches, "apply_lora_key_tolerance_patch", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
22	    monkeypatch.setattr(wgp_patches, "apply_lora_caching_patch", lambda *_a, **_k: True)
23	
24	    context_a = "test.patch.ctx.a"
25	    context_b = "test.patch.ctx.b"
26	
27	    try:
28	        wgp_patches.apply_all_wgp_patches(
29	            _make_dummy_wgp_module(),
30	            ".",
31	            context_id=context_a,
32	        )
33	        wgp_patches.apply_all_wgp_patches(
34	            _make_dummy_wgp_module(),
35	            ".",
36	            context_id=context_b,
37	        )
38	
39	        state_a = wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state(context_id=context_a)
40	        state_b = wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state(context_id=context_b)
41	
42	        assert "qwen_model_routing" in state_a
43	        assert "qwen_model_routing" in state_b
44	
45	        wgp_patches.clear_wgp_patch_context(context_a)
46	        assert wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state(context_id=context_a) == {}
47	        assert wgp_patches.get_wgp_patch_state(context_id=context_b)
48	    finally:
49	        wgp_patches.clear_wgp_patch_context(context_a)
50	        wgp_patches.clear_wgp_patch_context(context_b)
51

1	"""

4	Handles model loading, unloading, and dynamic model definition loading.

11	
12	from source.core.log import is_debug_enabled
13	from source.core.log.debug_card import DebugCard, render_card
14	from source.core.log.display_names import model_label
15	from source.core.log.model_runtime import generation_logger, model_logger
16	from source.runtime.wgp_bridge import (
17	    clear_wgp_loaded_model_state,
18	    get_model_recursive_prop,
19	    get_wgp_runtime_module,
20	    get_wgp_runtime_module_mutable,
21	    load_uni3c_controlnet,
22	    set_wgp_loaded_model_state,
23	    set_wgp_model_def,
24	    set_wgp_reload_needed,
25	    upsert_wgp_model_definition,
26	)
27	
28	
29	def load_missing_model_definition(orchestrator, model_key: str, json_path: str):
30	    """
31	    Dynamically load a missing model definition from JSON file.
32	    Replicates WGP's model loading logic for individual models.
33	
34	    Args:
35	        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance (provides wan_root)
36	        model_key: Model identifier string
37	        json_path: Path to the model definition JSON file
38	    """
39	    import json
40	    wgp = get_wgp_runtime_module_mutable()
41	
42	    with open(json_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
43	        try:
44	            json_def = json.load(f)
45	        except (ValueError, KeyError) as e:
46	            raise ValueError(f"Error while parsing Model Definition File '{json_path}': {str(e)}") from e
47	
48	    model_def = json_def["model"]
49	    model_def["path"] = json_path
50	    del json_def["model"]
51	    settings = json_def
52	
53	    existing_model_def = wgp.models_def.get(model_key, None)
54	    if existing_model_def is not None:
55	        existing_settings = existing_model_def.get("settings", None)
56	        if existing_settings is not None:
57	            existing_settings.update(settings)
58	        existing_model_def.update(model_def)
59	    else:
60	        set_wgp_model_def(model_key, model_def)
61	        model_def = upsert_wgp_model_definition(model_key, model_def, initialize=True)
62	        model_def["settings"] = settings
63	
64	
65	def load_model_impl(orchestrator, model_key: str) -> bool:
66	    """Load and validate a model type using WGP's exact generation-time pattern.
67	
68	    Args:
69	        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance
70	        model_key: Model identifier (e.g., "t2v", "vace_14B", "flux")
71	
72	    Returns:
73	        bool: True if a model switch actually occurred, False if already loaded
74	
75	    This replicates the exact model loading logic from WGP's generate_video function
76	    (lines 4249-4258) rather than the UI preloading function.
77	    """
78	    from source.models.wgp.generation_helpers import notify_worker_model_switch
79	
80	    if orchestrator.smoke_mode:
81	        # In smoke mode, skip heavy WGP model loading
82	        switched = model_key != orchestrator.current_model
83	        orchestrator.current_model = model_key
84	        orchestrator.state["model_type"] = model_key
85	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("SMOKE", f"Pretending to load model: {model_key}")
86	        return switched
87	
88	    if orchestrator._get_base_model_type(model_key) is None:
89	        raise ValueError(f"Unknown model: {model_key}")
90	
91	    wgp = get_wgp_runtime_module_mutable()
92	
93	    # Debug: Check if model definition is missing and diagnose why
94	    model_def = wgp.get_model_def(model_key)
95	    if model_def is None:
96	        available_models = list(wgp.models_def.keys())
97	        current_dir = os.getcwd()
98	        model_logger.warning(f"Model definition for '{model_key}' not found!")
99	        model_logger.debug(f"Current working directory: {current_dir}")
100	        model_logger.debug(f"Available models: {available_models}")
101	        model_logger.debug(f"Looking for model file: {orchestrator.wan_root}/defaults/{model_key}.json")
102	
103	        # Check if the JSON file exists and try to load it dynamically
104	        json_path = os.path.join(orchestrator.wan_root, "defaults", f"{model_key}.json")
105	        if os.path.exists(json_path):
106	            model_logger.warning(f"Model JSON file exists at {json_path} but wasn't loaded into models_def - attempting dynamic load")
107	            try:
108	                load_missing_model_definition(orchestrator, model_key, json_path)
109	                model_def = wgp.get_model_def(model_key)  # Try again after loading
110	                if model_def:
111	                    model_logger.success(f"Successfully loaded missing model definition for {model_key}")
112	                else:
113	                    model_logger.error(f"Failed to load model definition for {model_key} even after dynamic loading")
114	            except (RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
115	                model_logger.error(f"Failed to dynamically load model definition: {e}")
116	        else:
117	            model_logger.error(f"Model JSON file missing at {json_path}")
118	
119	    architecture = model_def.get('architecture') if model_def else 'unknown'
120	    modules = get_model_recursive_prop(model_key, "modules", return_list=True)
121	    model_logger.debug(f"Model Info: {model_key} | Architecture: {architecture} | Modules: {modules}")
122	
123	    # Use WGP's EXACT model loading pattern from generate_video (lines 4249-4258)
124	    # This is the SINGLE SOURCE OF TRUTH for whether a switch is needed
125	    switched = False
126	
127	    if model_key != wgp.transformer_type or wgp.reload_needed:
128	        model_logger.essential(
129	            f"Switching model: {model_label(wgp.transformer_type)} \u2192 {model_label(model_key)}"
130	        )
131	        switch_started_at = time.perf_counter()
132	        previous_model_label = model_label(wgp.transformer_type)
133	        next_model_label = model_label(model_key)
134	
135	        # Notify edge function BEFORE switch starts (worker will be busy loading)
136	        notify_worker_model_switch(old_model=wgp.transformer_type, new_model=model_key)
137	
138	        # Cleanup legacy collision-prone LoRAs BEFORE loading the new model.
139	        # This matches the original intent upstream of wgp.load_models() and avoids
140	        # accidental reuse of wrong/old LoRA artifacts across switches.
141	        try:
142	            from source.models.lora.lora_utils import cleanup_legacy_lora_collisions
143	            cleanup_legacy_lora_collisions()
144	        except (RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
145	            model_logger.warning(f"LoRA cleanup failed during model switch: {e}")
146	
147	        # Replicate WGP's exact unloading pattern (lines 4250-4254)
148	        current_offloadobj = getattr(wgp, "offloadobj", None)
149	        offload_reused = current_offloadobj is not None
150	        if current_offloadobj is not None:
151	            current_offloadobj.release()
152	        clear_wgp_loaded_model_state()
153	        gc.collect()
154	
155	        # CRITICAL: Clear CUDA cache after unloading to free reserved VRAM before loading new model
156	        # Without this, old model's reserved memory persists and new model OOMs during loading
157	        import torch
158	        if torch.cuda.is_available():
159	            torch.cuda.empty_cache()
160	            model_logger.debug("Cleared CUDA cache after model unload")
161	
162	        # Replicate WGP's exact loading pattern (lines 4255-4258)
163	        model_logger.debug(f"Loading model {next_model_label}...")
164	        wan_model, offloadobj = wgp.load_models(model_key)
165	        set_wgp_loaded_model_state(wan_model, offloadobj)
166	        model_logger.debug("Model loaded")
167	        set_wgp_reload_needed(False)
168	        switched = True
169	
170	        if is_debug_enabled():
171	            render_card(
172	                model_logger,
173	                DebugCard("Model Switch")
174	                .row("from", previous_model_label)
175	                .row("to", next_model_label)
176	                .row("offload_reuse", offload_reused)
177	                .row("duration_s", round(time.perf_counter() - switch_started_at, 2)),
178	                task_id=None,
179	            )
180	
181	        # Note: transformer_type is set automatically by load_models() at line 2929
182	    else:
183	        model_logger.debug("Model already loaded, no switch needed")
184	
185	    # Update our tracking to match WGP's state
186	    orchestrator.current_model = model_key
187	    orchestrator.state["model_type"] = model_key
188	    orchestrator.offloadobj = get_wgp_runtime_module().offloadobj  # Keep reference to WGP's offload object
189	
190	    if switched:
191	        model_logger.essential(f"Model loaded: {model_label(model_key)}")
192	
193	    return switched
194	
195	
196	def unload_model_impl(orchestrator):
197	    """Unload the current model using WGP's native unload function.
198	
199	    Args:
200	        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance
201	    """
202	    if orchestrator.smoke_mode:
203	        model_logger.debug_anomaly("SMOKE", f"Unload model: {orchestrator.current_model}")
204	        orchestrator.current_model = None
205	        orchestrator.offloadobj = None
206	        orchestrator.state["model_type"] = None
207	        orchestrator._cached_uni3c_controlnet = None
208	        return
209	    wgp = get_wgp_runtime_module_mutable()
210	
211	    if orchestrator.current_model and wgp.wan_model is not None:
212	        model_logger.essential(f"Unloading model: {model_label(orchestrator.current_model)}")
213	
214	        # Create a state object that WGP functions expect
215	        temp_state = {"model_type": orchestrator.current_model}
216	
217	        # Use WGP's native unload function
218	        try:
219	            wgp.unload_model_if_needed(temp_state)
220	
221	            # Clear our tracking
222	            orchestrator.current_model = None
223	            orchestrator.offloadobj = None
224	            orchestrator.state["model_type"] = None
225	            orchestrator._cached_uni3c_controlnet = None  # Clear Uni3C cache on model unload
226	
227	        except (RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
228	            model_logger.error(f"WGP unload_model_if_needed failed: {e}")
229	            raise
230	    else:
231	        model_logger.debug(f"📋 MODEL: No model to unload")
232	
233	
234	def get_or_load_uni3c_controlnet(orchestrator):
235	    """Get cached Uni3C controlnet or load it from disk.
236	
237	    This caches the controlnet across generations to avoid the ~2 minute
238	    disk load time for the 1.9GB checkpoint on each generation.
239	
240	    Args:
241	        orchestrator: WanOrchestrator instance (provides wan_root and cache)
242	
243	    Returns:
244	        WanControlNet instance or None if loading fails
245	    """
246	    if orchestrator._cached_uni3c_controlnet is not None:
247	        generation_logger.debug_anomaly("UNI3C_CACHE", "Using cached Uni3C controlnet (skipping disk load)")
248	        return orchestrator._cached_uni3c_controlnet
249	
250	    try:
251	        import torch
252	
253	        ckpts_dir = os.path.join(orchestrator.wan_root, "ckpts")
254	        generation_logger.debug_anomaly("UNI3C_CACHE", f"Loading Uni3C controlnet from disk (first use)...")
255	
256	        # load_uni3c_controlnet handles:
257	        # - base_dtype attribute setting
258	        # - per-layer dtype control (patch embeddings stay float32)
259	        # - proper model initialization
260	        controlnet = load_uni3c_controlnet(
261	            ckpts_dir=ckpts_dir,
262	            device="cuda",
263	            dtype=torch.float16,
264	            use_cache=False  # We manage our own cache
265	        )
266	        controlnet.eval()  # Ensure inference mode
267	
268	        # Cache for future generations
269	        orchestrator._cached_uni3c_controlnet = controlnet
270	        generation_logger.debug_anomaly("UNI3C_CACHE", f"Uni3C controlnet loaded and cached for future generations")
271	
272	        return controlnet
273	
274	    except (RuntimeError, OSError, ValueError) as e:
275	        generation_logger.debug_anomaly("UNI3C_CACHE", f"Failed to pre-load Uni3C controlnet: {e}")
276	        generation_logger.debug_anomaly("UNI3C_CACHE", "Falling back to on-demand loading in any2video")
277	        return None
278

58	        frame = sys._getframe(2)
59	    except ValueError:
60	        return False
61	    bridge_module = sys.modules.get("source.runtime.wgp_bridge")
62	    if bridge_module is None:
63	        return False
64	    return (
65	        frame.f_globals.get("wgp_bridge") is bridge_module
66	        or frame.f_locals.get("wgp_bridge") is bridge_module
67	    )
68	
69	
70	def _resolve_runtime_import_module(*, prefer_bridge: bool):
71	    if not prefer_bridge:
72	        return import_module
73	
74	    bridge_module = sys.modules.get("source.runtime.wgp_bridge")
75	    if bridge_module is None:
76	        return import_module
77	
78	    bridge_import_module = getattr(bridge_module, "import_module", None)
79	    if callable(bridge_import_module):
80	        return bridge_import_module
81	    return import_module
82	
83	
84	def _import_runtime_module(*, force_reload: bool, prefer_bridge_import: bool):
85	    module_name = str(_STATE["module_name"])
86	    if force_reload:
87	        sys.modules.pop(module_name, None)
88	    module = sys.modules.get(module_name)
89	    if module is None:
90	        module = _resolve_runtime_import_module(prefer_bridge=prefer_bridge_import)(module_name)
91	    _STATE["module_id"] = id(module)
92	    _STATE["import_count"] = int(_STATE["import_count"]) + 1
93	    _STATE["last_imported_at"] = time.time()
94	    _STATE["last_force_reload"] = bool(force_reload)
95	    return module
96	
97	
98	def _get_runtime_for_mutation(*, force_reload: bool = False):
99	    return _unwrap_runtime(get_wgp_runtime_module(force_reload=force_reload))
100	
101	
102	def get_wgp_runtime_state() -> dict[str, Any]:
103	    return dict(_STATE)
104	
105	
106	def reset_wgp_runtime_module() -> None:
107	    module_name = str(_STATE["module_name"])
108	    sys.modules.pop(module_name, None)
109	    _STATE.update({
110	        "module_id": None,
111	        "last_imported_at": None,
112	        "last_force_reload": True,
113	    })
114	
115	
116	def get_wgp_runtime_module(*, force_reload: bool = False):
117	    _bootstrap_runtime_paths()
118	    bridge_style_call = _called_via_public_wgp_bridge_api()
119	    module = _import_runtime_module(
120	        force_reload=force_reload,
121	        prefer_bridge_import=bridge_style_call,
122	    )
123	    if bridge_style_call:
124	        return module
125	    return _ReadonlyRuntimeProxy(module)
126	
127	
128	def get_wgp_runtime_module_mutable(*, force_reload: bool = False):
129	    _bootstrap_runtime_paths()
130	    return _import_runtime_module(force_reload=force_reload, prefer_bridge_import=False)
131	
132	
133	def get_wgp_models_def(*, force_reload: bool = False):
134	    return get_wgp_runtime_module(force_reload=force_reload).models_def
135	
136	
137	def set_wgp_model_def(model_name: str, model_def: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
138	    runtime = _get_runtime_for_mutation()
139	    runtime.models_def[model_name] = model_def
140	    if getattr(runtime, "transformer_type", None) == model_name and getattr(runtime, "wan_model", None) is not None:
141	        runtime.wan_model.model_def = model_def
142	    return model_def
143	
144	
145	def set_wgp_reload_needed(value: bool) -> bool:
146	    runtime = _get_runtime_for_mutation()
147	    runtime.reload_needed = bool(value)
148	    return runtime.reload_needed
149	
150

99	class WanOrchestrator:
100	    """Thin adapter around `wgp.generate_video` for easier programmatic use."""
101	
102	    # Typed runtime collaborator contract markers:
103	    # model_runtime: ModelRuntime | None = None
104	    # media_resolver: MediaResolver | None = None
105	    # parameter_resolver: ParameterResolver | None = None
106	    # self.model_runtime = model_runtime or DefaultModelRuntime(self)
107	    # runtime_context.prepare(...)
108	
109	    def __init__(self, wan_root: str, main_output_dir: Optional[str] = None):
110	        """Initialize orchestrator with WanGP directory.
111	
112	        Args:
113	            wan_root: Path to WanGP repository root directory (MUST be absolute path to Wan2GP/)
114	            main_output_dir: Optional path for output directory. If not provided, defaults to
115	                           'outputs' directory next to wan_root (preserves backwards compatibility)
116	
117	        IMPORTANT: Caller MUST have already changed to wan_root directory before calling this.
118	        wgp.py uses relative paths and expects to run from Wan2GP/.
119	        """
120	        import logging
121	        _init_logger = logging.getLogger('HeadlessQueue')
122	
123	        # Store the wan_root (should match current directory)
124	        self.wan_root = os.path.abspath(wan_root)
125	        current_dir = os.getcwd()
126	
127	        # CRITICAL CHECK: Verify caller already changed to the correct directory
128	        # wgp.py will import and execute module-level code that uses relative paths like "defaults/*.json"
129	        # If we're in the wrong directory, wgp will load 0 models and fail mysteriously
130	        if current_dir != self.wan_root:
131	            error_msg = (
132	                f"CRITICAL: WanOrchestrator must be initialized from Wan2GP directory!\n"
133	                f"  Current directory: {current_dir}\n"
134	                f"  Expected directory: {self.wan_root}\n"
135	                f"  Caller must chdir() before creating WanOrchestrator instance."
136	            )
137	            if is_debug_enabled():
138	                _init_logger.error(f"[INIT_DEBUG] {error_msg}")
139	            raise RuntimeError(error_msg)
140	
141	        # Verify Wan2GP structure
142	        if not os.path.isdir("defaults"):
143	            raise RuntimeError(
144	                f"defaults/ directory not found in {current_dir}. "
145	                f"This doesn't appear to be a valid Wan2GP directory!"
146	            )
147	        if not os.path.isdir("models"):
148	            if is_debug_enabled():
149	                _init_logger.warning(f"[INIT_DEBUG] models/ directory not found in {current_dir}")
150	
151	        # Ensure Wan2GP is first in sys.path so wgp.py imports correctly
152	        if self.wan_root in sys.path:
153	            sys.path.remove(self.wan_root)
154	        sys.path.insert(0, self.wan_root)
155	        # Optional smoke/CPU-only modes
156	        self.smoke_mode = bool(os.environ.get("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_SMOKE", ""))
157	        force_cpu = os.environ.get("HEADLESS_WAN2GP_FORCE_CPU", "0") == "1"
158	        init_summary = {
159	            "wan_root": self.wan_root,
160	            "cwd": current_dir,
161	            "smoke_mode": self.smoke_mode,
162	            "force_cpu": force_cpu,
163	        }
164	        if is_debug_enabled():
165	            _init_logger.debug("INIT_DEBUG %s", safe_dict_repr(init_summary))
166	
167	        # Force CPU if requested and guard CUDA capability queries before importing WGP
168	        if force_cpu and not os.environ.get("CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"):
169	            os.environ["CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES"] = "-1"
170	        try:
171	            import torch  # type: ignore
172	            # If CUDA isn't available, stub capability query used by upstream on import
173	            if force_cpu or not torch.cuda.is_available():
174	                try:
175	                    def _safe_get_device_capability(device=None):
176	                        return (8, 0)
177	                    torch.cuda.get_device_capability = _safe_get_device_capability  # type: ignore
178	                except (RuntimeError, AttributeError) as e:
179	                    _init_logger.debug("Failed to stub torch.cuda.get_device_capability: %s", e)
180	        except ImportError as e:
181	            _init_logger.debug("Failed to import torch for CPU/CUDA guards: %s", e)
182	
183	        # Force a headless Matplotlib backend to avoid Tkinter requirements during upstream imports
184	        if not os.environ.get("MPLBACKEND"):
185	            os.environ["MPLBACKEND"] = "Agg"
186	        try:
187	            import matplotlib  # type: ignore
188	            _orig_use = matplotlib.use  # type: ignore
189	            def _force_agg(_backend=None, *args, **kwargs):
190	                try:
191	                    return _orig_use('Agg', force=True)
192	                except (RuntimeError, ValueError):
193	                    return None
194	            matplotlib.use = _force_agg  # type: ignore
195	        except ImportError as e:
196	            _init_logger.debug("Failed to force matplotlib Agg backend: %s", e)
197	
198	        # Pre-import config hygiene for upstream wgp.py
199	        # Some older configs may store preload_model_policy as an int, but upstream now expects a list
200	        try:
201	            cfg_path = os.path.join(self.wan_root, "wgp_config.json")
202	            if os.path.isfile(cfg_path):
203	                import json as _json
204	                with open(cfg_path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as _r:
205	                    _cfg = _json.load(_r)
206	                changed = False
207	                pmp = _cfg.get("preload_model_policy", [])
208	                if isinstance(pmp, int):
209	                    _cfg["preload_model_policy"] = []  # disable preloading and fix type
210	                    changed = True
211	                # Ensure save paths exist and are strings
212	                if not isinstance(_cfg.get("save_path", ""), str):
213	                    _cfg["save_path"] = "outputs"
214	                    changed = True
215	                if not isinstance(_cfg.get("image_save_path", ""), str):
216	                    _cfg["image_save_path"] = "outputs"
217	                    changed = True
218	                if changed:
219	                    with open(cfg_path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as _w:
220	                        _json.dump(_cfg, _w, indent=4)
221	        except (OSError, ValueError, KeyError, TypeError):
222	            # Config hygiene should not block import
223	            pass
224	
225	        # Import WGP components after path setup (skip entirely in smoke mode)
226	        if not self.smoke_mode:
227	            try:
228	                current_dir = os.getcwd()
229	                if is_debug_enabled():
230	                    _init_logger.debug(
231	                        f"[INIT_DEBUG] {safe_dict_repr({'phase': 'import_wgp', 'cwd': current_dir, 'sys_path_0': sys.path[0] if sys.path else 'empty', 'wgp_in_modules': 'wgp' in sys.modules})}"
232	                    )
233	                # Double-check we're in the right directory before importing
234	                if current_dir != self.wan_root:
235	                    if is_debug_enabled():
236	                        _init_logger.error(f"[INIT_DEBUG] CRITICAL: Current directory {current_dir} != expected {self.wan_root}")
237	                    raise RuntimeError(f"Directory changed unexpectedly before wgp import: {current_dir} != {self.wan_root}")
238	
239	                # If wgp was previously imported from wrong directory, remove it so it reimports

243	                    reset_wgp_runtime_module()
